Implementation details for fill_effect:
```
def add_coordinate_effect(h, n, i, sign, dest):
    L=2*n-1
    # k=0
    dest[n-1] += sign * (1.0 - 2.0*h[i])
    # positive lags p=n..L-1
    fm = n-1-i
    if fm>0:
        dest[n:n+fm] += sign * (1.0 - h[i+1:i+fm+1])
    if i>0:
        # values h[i-k] for k=1..i: h[i-1::-1]
        dest[n:n+i] -= sign * h[i-1::-1]  # because effect includes -h; sign multiplies
    # negative lags
    if i>0:
        # p indices n-1-i : n-1 length i
        dest[n-1-i:n-1] += sign * (1.0 - h[0:i])
    fm = n-1-i
    if fm>0:
        # p indices i : n-1 length fm
        # values h[i+fm : i : -1]
        dest[i:n-1] += sign * (-1.0) * h[i+fm:i:-1]? Wait effect for negative right = -h[i+k]. sign * effect. sign * (-h) = -sign*h.
        dest[i:n-1] -= sign * h[i+fm:i:-1]
```
Check sign for positive right: effect -h; sign*effect = -sign*h. Good.
Negative left: effect +1-h; sign*(1-h).
Negative right: effect -h; sign*(-h) = -sign*h.
Need ensure slice lengths match:
- `dest[n:n+i] -= sign * h[i-1::-1]`: h[i-1::-1] length i if i>0. p range n to n+i-1 length i. Good.
- `dest[n-1-i:n-1] += sign*(1-h[0:i])`: length i. For i=n-1: slice dest[0:n-1]. Good.
- `dest[i:n-1] -= sign*h[i+fm:i:-1]`: fm=n-1-i, length fm. start index i, end i+fm=n-1. p range i..n-2 length fm. h slice from i+fm down to i+1 length fm. Good. For i=0, fm=n-1 -> dest[0:n-1] -= sign*h[n-1:0:-1] length n-1. Good.
Test for n=2,i=1: fm=0; i>0: positive right `dest[2:3] -= sign*h[0]`; negative left `dest[0:1] += sign*(1-h[0])`; negative right none. Good.

Line search details with exact lin:
```
def pair_improve(h,c,n,i,j,cmax,act_tol,max_evals=35):
    if i==j return 0
    lin=np.zeros(2*n-1)
    add_effect(h,n,i,+1,lin)
    add_effect(h,n,j,-1,lin)
    d=abs(i-j); idx1=n-1-d; idx2=n-1+d
    active = c >= cmax - act_tol
    l_act = lin[active]
    maxpd = l_act.max(); minpd = l_act.min()
    eps=1e-12
    if maxpd < -eps:
       low=0.0; high=min(h[j], 1-h[i])
       if high<=0: return
       side=1
    elif minpd > eps:
       high=0.0; low=max(-h[i], h[j]-1.0)
       if low>=0: return
       side=-1
    else:
       # maybe if one side active derivative slightly? Try both if maxpd<0? already. Else skip.
       # Could still if mixed but finite because active tol broad? Maybe skip.
       return
    # Convex bisection on [low,high] for side. Note for side positive low=0 high>0; negative low<0 high=0.
    a=low; b=high
    best_delta=0.0; best_val=cmax
    # evaluate function maybe at endpoints? We know endpoint derivative? We'll do loop.
    for it in range(max_evals):
       if b-a <= 1e-12: break
       m = 0.5*(a+b)
       sg_min, sg_max, val = sg_range(m, lin, c, idx1, idx2, d)
       if val < best_val: best_val=val; best_delta=m
       # decide
       if sg_max <= 1e-9: # derivative negative at m, min to right
           a=m
       elif sg_min >= -1e-9:
           b=m
       else:
           # 0 in range; m near optimum. Maybe shrink? break.
           # Actually if 0 in range, m is optimal (convex). But tolerance; break.
           break
       # Also update endpoint vals? maybe evaluate endpoints at end.
    # Evaluate endpoints and best_delta
```

```
def pair_optimizer(h,n,deadline,max_sweeps=100):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c=make_c(h,n)
    best_c=float(c.max())
    best_h=h.copy()
    improve_streak=0
    no_improve_candidate=0
    while time.time()<deadline:
       # quick recompute c? maintain
       cmax=best_c? We'll maintain c and cmax.
       # local segment: build E and process top candidates until no improvement or segment end
       segment_deadline = min(deadline, time.time()+60)
       improved_segment=0
       E=build_effect_matrix(h,n)
       updates=0
       while time.time()<segment_deadline:
          cmax = float(c.max())
          if cmax < best_c - 1e-15: best_c=cmax; best_h=h.copy()
          # active
          act_tol = max(1e-10, 1e-10*abs(cmax))
          active = np.flatnonzero(c >= cmax - act_tol)
          if len(active)==0: active=np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
          # For E matrix selection, need active <= maybe 8 for memory; if >8 take top 8 by c
          if len(active)>8:
             active = active[np.argsort(c[active])[-8:]]
          # Build score blocks maybe if n<=500 and len(active)*n*n not huge
          if n <= 500:
             Ea = E[:, active]
             diff = Ea[None,:,:] - Ea[:,None,:]
             # For each pair, positive descent if diff max <0, negative if diff min >0
             score = np.maximum(-diff.max(axis=2), diff.min(axis=2))
             np.fill_diagonal(score, -1e99)  # but score matrix
             flat_scores = score.ravel()
             # get top K indices where >tol
             thresh=1e-8
             cand_flat = np.flatnonzero(flat_scores > thresh)
             if len(cand_flat)==0:
                # If none with E, try random pairs maybe once
                ...
                improved_segment? break
             # Sort top up to K=200 by score
             if len(cand_flat)>200:
                # use argpartition
                part=np.argpartition(flat_scores[cand_flat], 200)[[:200]? Actually need indices.
            process pairs
          else:
             # random top gradient
          if improved_segment==0:
             break
       if improved_segment==0:
          # maybe try gradient pair random for 30s
          # if still no break
```

```
    c0=make_c(h0,n0); c0max=c0.max(); best_c=C(c0max,n0)

    # L-BFGS if time enough

       h_lb,c_lb = lbfgs_phase(h0,n0,t_lbfgs)

    # pair optimizer with remaining until deadline
    h_pair,c_pairsum = pair_optimizer(h_best,n0,deadline)
    if c_pairsum*2/n0 < best_c: update
    return best_h,best_c,best_n
```

```
def pair_optimizer(h0,n,deadline):
    h=project_feasible(h0,n); c=make_c(h,n); best_c=c.max(); best_h=h.copy()
    if time.time()>deadline: return best_h,best_c,2/n? maybe.
    max_active_for_matrix=8
    improve_streak=0
    no_quick=0
    while time.time()<deadline:
       # segment deadline maybe 30s or until no improvement
       seg_end=min(deadline, time.time()+45)
       E=None
       updates=0
       while time.time()<seg_end:
          cmax=float(c.max())
          if cmax < best_c - 1e-14: best_c=cmax; best_h=h.copy(); updates=0
          if cmax >= best_c + 1e-12: pass
          active_tol = max(1e-8, 1e-8*abs(cmax))
          active=np.flatnonzero(c >= cmax - active_tol)
          if len(active)==0: active=np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
          if len(active)>max_active:
             active = active[np.argsort(c[active])[-max_active:]]
          A=len(active)
          if E is None:
             E=build_effect_matrix(h,n)
          # generate candidates
          processed=0; improved=0
          if n*n*A <= 4_000_000:
             Ea=E[:,active]
             diff=Ea[None,:,:] - Ea[:,None,:]
             score=np.maximum(-diff.max(axis=2), diff.min(axis=2))
             iu,ju=np.triu_indices(n,1)
             score_tri=score[iu,ju]
             thr=1e-7
             cand=np.flatnonzero(score_tri > thr)
             if len(cand)==0:
                # perhaps random pairs
                ...
             else:
                sort top
             for idx in top:
                i,j=iu[idx],ju[idx]
                if time... break
                imprev,cnew = try_pair_move(h,c,n,i,j,cmax, 1e-8, max_evals=35)
                if imprev:
                   cmax=cnew; best update? try_pair updates c/h
                   improved+=1; updates+=1
                   if updates>=20:
                      break
                   if time... break
                processed+=1
                if processed>k_process: break
          else:
             # gradient top/random
          if updates>=20 or processed>=k_process:
             continue # rebuild E next segment? within same seg, could break inner to rebuild
          if improved==0:
             # try random force pairs maybe 50
             ...
             if no_random_improve: break
       if time... break
       if no improvement in segment:
          # maybe L-BFGS small? or break
          # try random no-improve count; if 2 break.
    # periodic recompute c? We update exact; but to remove drift:
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c=make_c(h,n)
    cmax=c.max()
    if cmax < best_c: best equals
    else if h changed from best, use best_h/best_c from before? Need ensure best_h corresponds to best_c. We store best_h when improvement. At end return best_h,best_c,2/n? It can be caller.
```

Pseudo code:

```
def pair_value_delta(delta, lin, c, idx1, idx2, d):
    v = c + delta*lin
    if d != 0:
       dd=delta*delta
       v[idx1]+=dd; v[idx2]+=dd
    return float(v.max())

def active_sg(delta, lin, c, idx1, idx2, d, sg_tol):
    v=c + delta*lin
    if d:
      dd=delta*delta; v[idx1]+=dd; v[idx2]+=dd
    val=float(v.max())
    active = v >= val - sg_tol
    # derivative lin + 2delta q
    # Instead of copy full, use lin[active] plus if active contains idx
    sgv = lin[active].copy()
    if d:
       # find positions where active true and idx
       # Since active is boolean, can check:
       if active[idx1]:
           # Need add to the element of sgv corresponding to idx1. But sgv is 1D; need index in active.
           # Could compute positions:
           none easy. Simpler: full derivative:
    full_sg = lin.copy()
    if d: full_sg[idx1]+=2δ; full_sg[idx2]+=2δ
    return float(full_sg[active].min()), float(full_sg[active].max()), val
```

- sg values = lin[active]; if d and active[idx1], need add 2δ to corresponding sg. We can get pos = np.flatnonzero(active); then mask = (pos==idx1)|(pos==idx2); sgv[mask]+=2δ. This alloc pos but okay. Or full copy simpler. n small. Use full copy.

Since effect_j negative right = -h; -effect=+h. Good.

```
Positive left fm = n-1-i. If i=n-1, fm=0. If i=0, fm=n-1, dest[n:L] length n-1.
```

def build_effect_matrix(h,n):
 L=2*n-1
 E=np.zeros((n,L),float)
 for i in range(n):
   add_effect(h,n,i,1.0,E[i])
 return E

def smooth_obj_grad(x,n,alpha,mu):
 c=np.correlate(x,1-x,'full')
 weights=soft_weights(c,alpha)
 g=grad_from_weights(x,n,c,weights)
 g += 2*mu*(x.sum()-n/2)
 return g

m=c.max(); s=alpha*(c-m); mx=s.max(); if mx>500: e=np.exp(s-mx); else e=np.exp(s); e/=e.sum(); return e
```
`grad_from_weights`:
```
def grad_from_weights(h,n,weights):
 g=np.zeros(n)
 for m,w in enumerate(weights):
   if w < 1e-14: continue
   k=m-(n-1)
   if k>=0:
      nv=n-k
      g[:nv]+=w*(1-h[k:])
      g[k:]-=w*h[:nv]
   else:
      kk=-k; nv=n-kk
      g[kk:]+=w*(1-h[:nv])
      g[:nv]-=w*h[kk:]
 return g
```
This matches derivative of sum c weighted. For k=0, both slices same: g[:n]+=w(1-h); g[0:n]-=w h -> w(1-2h). Good.

Alpha choices. Since c sum ~50, to focus active need α large. Previous α up to 2e6. We'll use [1000,10000,100000,1e6]. Use `mu` maybe 10. For n=300, okay.
```

Let's define `pair_optimizer` exact code with time guards.

```
def pair_optimizer(h0,n,deadline):
    h=project_feasible(h0,n)
    c=make_c(h,n); cmax=float(c.max()); best_h=h.copy(); best_c=cmax
    if cmax*(2/n) > best? no.
    maxA=6 if n>400 else 8
    max_top=250
    processed_limit=3000 per rebuild? Actually top candidates 250 enough.
    no_improve_segments=0
    while time.time() < deadline:
        seg_start=time.time()
        seg_end=min(deadline, seg_start+40)
        E=None
        segment_improved=False
        while time.time() < seg_end:
            cmax=float(c.max())
            if cmax < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_c=cmax; best_h=h.copy(); segment_improved=True
            if cmax > best_c + 1e-8:
                h=best_h.copy(); c=make_c(h,n); cmax=best_c; E=None; break
            # active
            act_tol=max(1e-8, 1e-9*abs(cmax))? Let's set 1e-7? For candidate selection, use 1e-6? Hmm. For exact active, `try_pair_move` uses 1e-8. For E selection, use maybe 10*try tol to include near active. Use `sel_tol=max(1e-6, 1e-6*abs(cmax))`? cmax 50 =>5e-5. C 3e-7, okay maybe includes near. But if too broad, scores from nonactive may mislead. Fine.
            active=np.flatnonzero(c >= cmax - sel_tol)
            if len(active)==0: active=np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
            if len(active)>maxA: active=active[np.argsort(c[active])[-maxA:]]
            if E is None: E=build_effect_matrix(h,n)
            # candidate processing
            top_candidates=[]
            if n*n*len(active) <= 5_000_000:
               Ea=E[:,active]
               diff=Ea[None,:,:] - Ea[:,None,:]
               score=np.maximum(-diff.max(axis=2), diff.min(axis=2))
               iu,ju=np.triu_indices(n,1)
               score_tri=score[iu,ju]
               # sort top
               k=min(max_top, len(score_tri))
               # If score_tri all zeros? use argmax
               if k>0:
                  # use argpartition for top k
                  part=np.argpartition(-score_tri, k-1)[:k]
                  order=part[np.argsort(-score_tri[part])]
                  for p in order:
                     if score_tri[p] > 1e-8:
                         top_candidates.append((iu[p],ju[p]))
                     else: break
            else:
               # fallback random based on smooth gradient
               g=grad_from_weights(h,n,soft_weights(c,50000.0))
               low=np.argsort(g)[:20]; high=np.argsort(g)[-20:]
               for a in low[:10]:
                  for b in high[:10]:
                     if a!=b: top_candidates.append((a,b))
            if not top_candidates:
               # small random
               for _ in range(30):
                   i=int(rng? no rng passed?)
```

```
def soft_weights(c, alpha):
   m=c.max(); s=alpha*(c-m); mx=s.max()
   if mx>500: e=np.exp(s-mx); w=e/e.sum()
   else: e=np.exp(s); w=e/e.sum()
   return w
```
If α small and s large negative, exp underflow okay. If α large positive values all near 0 but some -large, exp stable.

Potential bug in `project_feasible`: For odd n target .5, iterative scaling with room. Implement robust:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    target=0.5*n
    for _ in range(200):
        s=h.sum(); diff=target-s
        if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
        if diff>0:
            room=1.0-h; tot=room.sum()
            if tot<1e-15: break
            h += diff*room/tot
        else:
            tot=h.sum()
            if tot<1e-15: break
            h += diff*h/tot
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # final exact correction
    s=h.sum(); diff=target-s
    if abs(diff)>1e-14:
       # distribute to variable with most room
       if diff>0:
          idx=np.argmax(1-h)
          h[idx]=min(1.0,h[idx]+diff)
       else:
          idx=np.argmax(h)
          h[idx]=max(0.0,h[idx]+diff)
    return h
```
If diff large, one coordinate may not absorb; loop again? Add while few:
```
for _ in range(10):
 s=h.sum(); diff=target-s
 if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
 if diff>0:
   idxs=np.where(h<1.0)[0]; if none break; idx=idxs.argmax? choose index with max room; h[idx]=min(1,h[idx]+diff)
 else: ...
```
Good.

`resample_h`:
```
def resample_h(h,n_old,n_new):
 if n_old==n_new: return h.copy()
 x_old=(np.arange(n_old)+0.5)*2.0/n_old
 x_new=(np.arange(n_new)+0.5)*2.0/n_new
 hi=np.interp(x_new,x_old,h, left=h[0], right=h[-1])
 return project_feasible(hi,n_new)
```

Let's write `try_pair_move` in detail with bounds exactly.
```
def try_pair_move(h, c, n, i, j, cmax, max_evals=35, act_tol=None, force=False):
    if i == j: return False, None
    hi = float(h[i]); hj=float(h[j])
    low = max(-hi, hj - 1.0)
    high = min(hj, 1.0 - hi)
    if high - low < 1e-13: return False, None
    L = 2*n - 1
    d = abs(i-j)
    idx1 = n - 1 - d
    idx2 = n - 1 + d
    lin = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    add_effect(h, n, i, 1.0, lin)
    add_effect(h, n, j, -1.0, lin)
    if act_tol is None:
        act_tol = max(1e-9, 1e-9 * abs(float(cmax)))
    active = np.flatnonzero(c >= cmax - act_tol)
    if active.size == 0:
        active = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)
    l = lin[active]
    max_pd = float(l.max()); min_pd = float(l.min())
    side = 0
    if max_pd < -1e-11:
        side = 1
    elif min_pd > 1e-11:
        side = -1
    if side == 0:
        if not force:
            return False, None
        if max_pd < -1e-13:
            side = 1
        elif min_pd > 1e-13:
            side = -1
        else:
            return False, None
    if side > 0:
        a = 0.0; b = high
        if b <= a + 1e-13: return False,None
    else:
        a = low; b = 0.0
        if b <= a + 1e-13: return False,None
    best_delta = 0.0
    best_val = float(cmax)
    # Evaluate b (bound)
    val_b = pair_value_bisection(b, lin, c, idx1, idx2, d)
    if val_b < best_val:
        best_val = val_b; best_delta = b
    # Also if side negative, a bound maybe has value? Evaluate a? At side negative, a=low maybe not 0. But we know f at 0=cmax; if f decreasing as δ increases (toward 0) or? side negative means min at a (negative) <0; evaluate low bound.
    val_a = pair_value_bisection(a, lin, c, idx1, idx2, d)
    if val_a < best_val:
        best_val = val_a; best_delta = a
    for _ in range(max_evals):
        if b - a <= 1e-12: break
        m = 0.5*(a+b)
        sgn_min, sgn_max, val = pair_sg_bisect(m, lin, c, idx1, idx2, d, act_tol)
        if val < best_val:
            best_val = val; best_delta = m
        if sgn_max <= 1e-8:
            a = m
        elif sgn_min >= -1e-8:
            b = m
        else:
            break
    # candidates
    candidates = (0.0, best_delta, a, b, low, high)
    for delta in candidates:
        if delta < low - 1e-12 or delta > high + 1e-12:
            continue
        if abs(delta) < 1e-14:
            continue
        val = pair_value_bisection(delta, lin, c, idx1, idx2, d)
        if val < best_val:
            best_val = val; best_delta = delta
    if best_val >= cmax - 1e-9:
        return False, None
    delta = best_delta
    # clamp
    if delta > high: delta = high
    if delta < low: delta = low
    if abs(delta) < 1e-13: return False,None
    old_i = hi; old_j = hj
    new_i = old_i + delta; new_j = old_j - delta
    if new_i < 0.0:
        new_i = 0.0; delta = old_i; new_j = old_j - delta
    elif new_i > 1.0:
        new_i = 1.0; delta = 1.0 - old_i; new_j = old_j - delta
    if new_j < 0.0:
        new_j = 0.0; delta = old_j; new_i = old_i + delta
    elif new_j > 1.0:
        new_j = 1.0; delta = old_j - 1.0; new_i = old_i + delta
    actual = float(delta)
    if abs(actual) < 1e-13:
        return False, None
    c_trial = c + actual * lin
    if d != 0:
        dd = actual * actual
        c_trial[idx1] += dd
        c_trial[idx2] += dd
    nm = float(c_trial.max())
    if nm < cmax - 1e-9:
        h[i] = new_i
        h[j] = new_j
        c[:] = c_trial
        return True, nm
    return False, None
```
But careful: We call `pair_value_bisection(a, ...)` where a may be 0 for side positive or low for side negative. If a=0, returns cmax (plus maybe quad at idx? δ=0). Good. Side negative a=low bound, may be <0. Good.
Candidate low/high original side bounds? For side positive low=0 high=high; for negative low=low high=0. We include both.

`pair_value_bisection` name maybe `pair_value`.
```
def pair_value(delta, lin, c, idx1, idx2, d):
    v = c + delta*lin
    if d:
       dd=delta*delta; v[idx1]+=dd; v[idx2]+=dd
    return float(v.max())
```
`pair_sg`:
```
def pair_sg_delta(delta, lin, c, idx1, idx2, d, act_tol):
    v = c + delta*lin
    if d:
       dd=delta*delta; v[idx1]+=dd; v[idx2]+=dd
    val=float(v.max())
    active = v >= val - act_tol
    sg = lin.copy()
    if d:
       t=2.0*delta; sg[idx1]+=t; sg[idx2]+=t
    sgv=sg[active]
    return float(sgv.min()), float(sgv.max()), val
```
If active empty? impossible. If v.max() NaN? no.

Could optimize by reusing v from endpoint? Not needed.

Potential numerical: `act_tol` in pair_sg c units; if active includes all due tol too large? Maybe. Use same as derivative ~1e-8. Fine.

`lbfgs_obj` and grad need handle x not clipped? Bounds enforce [0,1] but L-BFGS may evaluate within. Sum penalty. Good.
```
def smooth_max_c5(x, n, alpha):
    c = np.correlate(x, 1.0-x, mode='full')
    y = c * (2.0/n)
    m = float(y.max())
    s = alpha*(y-m)
    mx = float(s.max())
    if mx > 500:
        v = (mx + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(s-mx))))/alpha
    else:
        v = np.log(np.sum(np.exp(s)))/alpha
    return m + v
```
Actually if mx>500, logsum = mx + log(sum exp(s-mx)); smooth = (logsum)/alpha. Good.
```
def lbfgs_obj(x,n,alpha,mu):
    val=smooth_max_c5(x,n,alpha)
    val += mu*(x.sum()-0.5*n)**2
    return val
```
Gradient:
```
y=c*2/n; weights=soft_weights(y,alpha); g = (2/n)*grad_from_weights(x,n,weights); g += 2mu...
```
Need if x has nan? not.

`grad_from_weights` weights sum 1; uses h x. If x near bounds. Good.

Now, `base_starts` top-level. It needs rng. We can generate starts only if n small; use project.
```
def make_starts(n, rng=None):
  x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2/n
  starts=[]
  starts.append(np.full(n,0.5))
  tri=np.clip(1.0-np.abs(x-1.0),0.0,1.0)
  starts.append(tri)
  tri2=np.power(np.clip(1.0-np.abs(x-1.0),0.0,1.0),2.0)
  starts.append(tri2)
  cosv=np.clip(0.5+0.5*np.cos(np.pi*(x-1.0)),0.0,1.0) # center 1, edges 0; area ~1? project
  starts.append(cosv)
  gauss=np.exp(-2.0*(x-1.0)**2); starts.append(gauss)
  if rng is not None:
    for amp in [0.05,0.1,0.2]:
       h=0.5 + amp*rng.standard_normal(n)
       starts.append(h)
  out=[]
  for h in starts:
     hp=project_feasible(h,n)
     out.append(hp)
  return out
```
We may duplicate uniform. In run, for small n, choose starts with raw c lower. Evaluate all, sort, optimize top 3.
Actually uniform n gives C=0.5, not best. But optimization from uniform maybe baseline.

```
    test_ns=[]
    for m in [max(50, n0//2), max(100, n0-50), min(800, n0+100), min(1000, 2*n0)]:

    # small starts

       evals.sort(key=lambda z:z[0]) # lambda not allowed! Need no lambdas. Use sort with key? Lambdas disallowed. Can use `evals.sort()` tuple sorts by first. Good.
       per2 = max(15, min(60, int((deadline - time.time() - 30)//max(1,len(small_ns)*2))))
       for _,s in evals[:2]:
          if time.time()>deadline-10: break
          alloc=min(per2, deadline-time.time()-10)
          if alloc<10: break
          hopt,copt=optimize_candidate(s,n,time.time()+alloc,rng)
          hp=project_feasible(hopt,n); cproj=compute_c5_exact(hp,n)
          if cproj<best_c: update
 # if time > 30 after all, final polish best with pair_optimizer

    hp=project_feasible(hopt,best_n); cproj=compute_c5_exact(hp,best_n)

```

`is_feasible_raw` top-level:
```
def is_feasible_raw(h,n):
 target=0.5*n
 return (np.min(h)>=-1e-12 and np.max(h)<=1.0+1e-12 and abs(h.sum()-target)<=1e-8)
```

Let's write `pair_optimizer` with no L-BFGS. Need pass `rng` default.

Implement pair_optimizer inner:
```
def pair_optimizer(h0,n,deadline,rng=None):
    if rng is None: rng = np.random.RandomState(12345)
    h=project_feasible(h0,n)
    c=make_c(h,n)
    cmax=float(c.max())
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=cmax
    if time.time() >= deadline: return best_h,best_c
    no_improve_rounds=0
    while time.time() < deadline:
        seg_start=time.time()
        seg_end=min(deadline, seg_start + 30.0)
        E=None
        segment_improved=False
        while time.time() < seg_end:
            cmax = float(c.max())
            if cmax < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_c = cmax; best_h = h.copy(); segment_improved=True
            if cmax > best_c + 1e-7:
                h = best_h.copy(); c = make_c(h,n); cmax=best_c; E=None; break
            sel_tol = max(1e-8, 1e-9*abs(cmax))
            active = np.flatnonzero(c >= cmax - sel_tol)
            if active.size == 0:
                active=np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)
            deny = n*n
            maxA = max(1, min(8, 1200000 // deny)) if deny>0 else 1
            if active.size > maxA:
                order = active[np.argsort(c[active])[-maxA:]]
                active = order
            if E is None:
                E = build_effect_matrix(h,n)
            top_candidates=[]
            A=len(active)
            if n*n*A <= 1200000:
                Ea = E[:, active]
                diff = Ea[None,:,:] - Ea[:,None,:]
                score = np.maximum(-diff.max(axis=2), diff.min(axis=2))
                iu, ju = np.triu_indices(n,1)
                score_tri = score[iu,ju]
                k = min(200, score_tri.size)
                if k > 0:
                    if k == score_tri.size:
                        order = np.argsort(-score_tri)
                    else:
                        part = np.argpartition(-score_tri, k-1)[:k]
                        order = part[np.argsort(-score_tri[part])]
                    for p in order:
                        if score_tri[p] > 1e-8:
                            top_candidates.append((int(iu[p]), int(ju[p])))
                        else:
                            break
            # fallback if none
            if not top_candidates:
                y = c * (2.0/n)
                w = soft_weights(y, 50000.0)
                g = grad_from_weights(h,n,w)
                order = np.argsort(g)
                low = order[:min(30,n//3+1)]
                high = order[-min(30,n//3+1):]
                for aidx in low:
                    for bidx in high:
                        if aidx != bidx:
                            top_candidates.append((int(aidx), int(bidx)))
                if len(top_candidates) == 0 and n > 1:
                    for _ in range(50):
                        ii=int(rng.randint(n)); jj=int(rng.randint(n))
                        if ii != jj: top_candidates.append((ii,jj))
            if not top_candidates:
                break
            processed=0; accepted_segment=0
            for ii,jj in top_candidates:
                if time.time() >= seg_end: break
                # quick: if bounds impossible both ways
                if (h[ii] <= 1e-15 and h[jj] <= 1e-15) or (h[ii] >= 1-1e-15 and h[jj] >= 1-1e-15):
                    continue
                improved, cnew = try_pair_move(h,c,n,ii,jj,cmax, max_evals=32, act_tol=sel_tol)
                if improved:
                    cmax = cnew
                    if cmax < best_c - 1e-14:
                        best_c = cmax; best_h = h.copy(); segment_improved=True
                    accepted_segment += 1
                    if accepted_segment >= 20:
                        # recompute and rebuild
                        cdir = make_c(h,n)
                        ma = float(cdir.max())
                        if ma < cmax - 1e-12:
                            c = cdir; cmax = ma
                            if cmax < best_c - 1e-14:
                                best_c = cmax; best_h = h.copy(); segment_improved=True
                        elif ma > best_c + 1e-7:
                            h = best_h.copy(); c = make_c(h,n); cmax = best_c
                        E = None
                        break
                processed += 1
                if processed >= 800: break
            # If processed all and no accepted_segment? E still same. If segment_improved false break inner? The while top continues but no candidates? We'll if accepted_segment==0 and processed>=len(top_candidates)? Simpler: if accepted_segment == 0:
            if accepted_segment == 0:
                # If processed many or all, break inner; maybe try next segment? E same, will no improve. Break.
                break
            # If accepted >0 but maybe candidates exhausted; continue inner to next rebuild? E may be stale.
            if accepted_segment > 0 and processed > 0 and time.time() < seg_end:
                # if we processed all top_candidates without exceeding accepted limit, break to rebuild? E stale slightly; could continue with same E? Better rebuild if processed all or time.
                # We'll break inner to outer to rebuild E in next segment if time.
                break
        if not segment_improved:
            no_improve_rounds += 1
            # Give up if 2 no improve segments
            if no_improve_rounds >= 2:
                break
        else:
            no_improve_rounds = 0
    # recompute best_h c exact
    cdir = make_c(best_h,n)
    best_c = float(cdir.max())
    return best_h, best_c
```

Let's write `lbfgs_phase` return csum. It uses `best_c5` but could return csum.
```
def lbfgs_phase(h0,n,deadline):
 h=project_feasible(h0,n)
 c=make_c(h,n); best_csum=float(c.max()); best_h=h.copy()
 if time.time() >= deadline or n>800: return best_h,best_csum
 bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
 configs=[(500.0,10.0),(5000.0,50.0),(50000.0,200.0)]
 if n<=400: configs.append((500000.0,1000.0))
 for alpha,mu in configs:
   if time.time() >= deadline: break
   maxiter=80 if n<=400 else 40
   try:
      res=minimize(_lbfgs_obj, h, jac=_lbfgs_grad, args=(n,alpha,mu), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-8,'maxfun':maxiter*10,'maxls':50})
      hnew=project_feasible(res.x,n)
      cnew=make_c(hnew,n); cs=float(cnew.max())
      if cs < best_csum - 1e-12:
         best_csum=cs; best_h=hnew.copy(); h=hnew
   except Exception:
      pass
 return best_h,best_csum
```

So:
```
def soft_weights(y, alpha):
    s=alpha*(y - y.max())
    e=np.exp(s)
    return e / e.sum()
def smooth_max_c5(x,n,alpha):
    c=np.correlate...
    y=c*(2.0/n); m=float(y.max()); s=alpha*(y-m); return m + float(np.log(np.sum(np.exp(s))))/alpha
```

Detailed `run`:
```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 8.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n0 = int(len(h0))
    except Exception:
        h0 = np.full(300, 0.5)
        n0 = 300
    if n0 < 2:
        h0 = np.full(2,0.5); n0=2
    h0 = project_feasible(h0,n0)
    best_h = h0.copy(); best_c = compute_c5_exact(best_h,n0); best_n=n0
    # sym
    hs = project_sym_feasible(h0,n0)
    cs = compute_c5_exact(hs,n0)
    if cs < best_c:
       best_h=hs.copy(); best_c=cs; best_n=n0
    # main
    reserve = max(120.0, 0.30*budget_s)
    main_deadline = deadline - reserve
    if main_deadline < t0 + 20.0:
       main_deadline = deadline - 2.0
    h1,c1 = optimize_candidate(best_h, best_n, main_deadline, rng)
    hp=project_feasible(h1,best_n)
    cp=compute_c5_exact(hp,best_n)
    if cp < best_c:
       best_h=hp; best_c=cp; best_n=best_n
    # maybe if raw best_h feasible and exact lower than projected?
    if is_feasible_raw(h1,best_n):
       cr=compute_c5_exact(h1,best_n)
       if cr < best_c:
          best_h=h1; best_c=cr
    # tests
    if time.time() < deadline - 30:
       test_candidates=[]
       cur=best_n
       for d in [1,2,3,5,10,20,cur//10]:
          if d<=0: continue
          for m in [cur-d, cur+d]:
             if 20 <= m <= 1000 and m != best_n:
                if m not in test_candidates: test_candidates.append(m)
       for m in [best_n//2, best_n*2, 150, 600]:
          if 20 <= m <= 1000 and m != best_n and m not in test_candidates:
             test_candidates.append(m)
       # limit based on time
       remaining = deadline - time.time() - 20
       max_tests = max(0, int(remaining / 35.0))
       test_candidates = test_candidates[:min(max_tests, 8)]
       for n_try in test_candidates:
          if time.time() > deadline - 20: break
          hseed = resample_h(best_h, best_n, n_try)
          alloc = min(70.0, deadline - time.time() - 15.0)
          if alloc < 20.0: break
          ho,co = optimize_candidate(hseed, n_try, time.time()+alloc, rng)
          hp = project_feasible(ho,n_try)
          cp = compute_c5_exact(hp,n_try)
          if cp < best_c:
             best_h=hp; best_c=cp; best_n=n_try
          elif is_feasible_raw(ho,n_try):
             cr=compute_c5_exact(ho,n_try)
             if cr < best_c:
                best_h=ho; best_c=cr; best_n=n_try
    # small starts if time
    if time.time() < deadline - 40:
       small_ns = [n for n in [5,7,10,15,20,30,50,100,150] if n != best_n]
       for n_try in small_ns:
          if time.time() > deadline - 20: break
          starts = make_starts(n_try, rng)
          vals=[]
          for idx,s in enumerate(starts):
             vals.append((compute_c5_exact(s,n_try), idx))
          vals.sort()
          per = max(15.0, min(40.0, (deadline - time.time() - 30.0)/2.0))
          for sc,idx in vals[:2]:
             if time.time() > deadline - 15: break
             alloc = min(per, deadline - time.time() - 10.0)
             if alloc < 12.0: break
             hseed=starts[idx]
             ho,co=optimize_candidate(hseed,n_try,time.time()+alloc,rng)
             hp=project_feasible(ho,n_try)
             cp=compute_c5_exact(hp,n_try)
             if cp < best_c:
                best_h=hp; best_c=cp; best_n=n_try
             elif is_feasible_raw(ho,n_try):
                cr=compute_c5_exact(ho,n_try)
                if cr < best_c:
                   best_h=ho; best_c=cr; best_n=n_try
    # final polish if time
    if time.time() < deadline - 5:
       ho,co = pair_optimizer(best_h,best_n,deadline-2,rng)
       hp=project_feasible(ho,best_n)
       cp=compute_c5_exact(hp,best_n)
       if cp < best_c:
          best_h=hp; best_c=cp
       elif is_feasible_raw(ho,best_n):
          cr=compute_c5_exact(ho,best_n)
          if cr < best_c:
             best_h=ho; best_c=cr
    # final project
    h_final = project_feasible(best_h,best_n)
    c_final = compute_c5_exact(h_final,best_n)
    if is_feasible_raw(best_h,best_n):
       c_raw=compute_c5_exact(best_h,best_n)
       if c_raw < c_final:
          h_final=best_h; c_final=c_raw
    else:
       h_final=h_final
    return (h_final, float(c_final), int(len(h_final)))
```

```
def optimize_candidate(h0,n,deadline,rng=None):
    h=project_feasible(h0,n)
    c=make_c(h,n); cs0=float(c.max()); c50=cs0*2/n
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c5=c50; best_cs=cs0
    if time.time() >= deadline: return best_h,best_c5
    t1 = min(deadline, time.time()+150.0)
    h1,cs1=pair_optimizer(h,n,t1,rng)
    c51=cs1*2/n
    if c51 < best_c5 - 1e-15:
       best_h=h1; best_c5=c51; best_cs=cs1
    # L-BFGS
    if n <= 800 and deadline - time.time() > 50:
       t = min(deadline, time.time()+35.0)
       h2,cs2=lbfgs_phase(best_h,n,t)
       c52=cs2*2/n
       if c52 < best_c5 - 1e-15: best...
       # perturb
       if deadline - time.time() > 25 and rng is not None:
           noise = 0.003 if n<=500 else 0.001
           hp = best_h + noise*(rng.standard_normal(n)-0.5)
           hp = project_feasible(hp,n)
           h3,cs3=lbfgs_phase(hp,n,min(deadline,time.time()+20.0))
           c53=cs3*2/n
           if c53 < best_c5 - 1e-15: best...
    # pair final
    h4,cs4=pair_optimizer(best_h,n,deadline,rng)
    c54=cs4*2/n
    if c54 < best_c5 - 1e-15: best...
    return best_h,best_c5
```

```
def make_starts(n,rng=None):
 x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/n)
 starts=[]
 starts.append(np.full(n,0.5))
 tri=np.clip(1.0-np.abs(x-1.0),0.0,1.0)
 starts.append(tri.copy())
 p=2.0; starts.append(np.power(tri,p))
 starts.append(np.power(tri,0.5))
 starts.append(np.power(tri,4.0))
 cosv=np.clip(0.5+0.5*np.cos(np.pi*(x-1.0)),0.0,1.0); starts.append(cosv)
 gauss=np.exp(-2.0*(x-1.0)*(x-1.0)); starts.append(gauss)
 if rng is not None:
   amps=[0.03,0.08,0.15]
   for a in amps:
      h=0.5 + a*(rng.standard_normal(n)-0.5)
      starts.append(h)
 out=[]
 for h in starts:
   out.append(project_feasible(h,n))
 return out
```

One subtlety: The line objective f(δ) is convex only if quadratic coefficients are nonnegative. We have +δ^2 at two lags. Yes. But what about if a lag's coefficient could be negative? Pair update of h_i h_j product gives +δ^2 due h(1-h) = h - h_i h_j; product change has +δ^2 (because one variable increases, one decreases, product decreases by? We derived +δ^2). If both coordinates in same factor (1-h_sh) maybe product? Let's re-derive: T = sum left h - sum h_left h_right. If coordinate i +δ (left), j -δ (right). Term = left +? If i left, j right: left'=l+δ, right'=r-δ. T term = l+δ - (l+δ)(r-δ) = l - lr + δ - lδ + rδ + δ^2? Wait expand (l+δ)(r-δ)=lr -lδ + rδ - δ^2. T = l+δ - lr + lδ - rδ + δ^2 = original + δ + (l-r)δ + δ^2. Quadratic +1. Good. If i right, j left (i>j): analogous T= r+? original + δ(l-r?) + δ^2. Always +. Could there be lag where both coordinates appear in two different terms (e.g., i left in one term, j right in another term) for same shift? For a given shift k, if |i-j|=k, then they are in same term if j=i+k for positive or i=j+k for negative. Could they also both appear in different terms within the same sum? For a fixed k, coordinate i appears in at most two terms: as left with right i+k, and as right with left i-k. Coordinate j similarly. If |i-j|=k, say j=i+k. Then i appears as left with j (cross), and possibly as right with left i-k. j appears as right with i (cross), and possibly as left with right j+k. These are two distinct terms within same c_k: term p=i (both) and term p=i-k? Actually if i-k ≥0, coordinate i appears as right in term p=i-k (h_{i-k} q_i), coordinate j appears not. Coordinate j appears as left in term p=j (h_j q_{j+k}) if j+k<n, coordinate i not. So i and j appear in their own separate single-coordinate terms plus one cross term. Linear contributions from separate terms captured by individual effects; no quadratic between separate terms. Quadratic only cross. Good.

To be safe, use `k = min(500, score_tri.size)` instead of 200. 500 line checks okay. Processed limit 800. If no improvement among top 500, likely local. Let's set `k = min(400, score_tri.size)` maybe. Fallback gradient 900. Good.
Also `processed >= 800` covers. We'll use 400.

If no improvement but scores top >1e-8, maybe due line search tolerance; could do random top 200 with force=True to search maybe. Add after exact no improvements? In pair_optimizer when accepted_segment==0, we could do a forced random batch:
```
if accepted_segment == 0:
   # try 50 random/gradient with force=True maybe
   for ... top_candidates: try_pair_move(force=True)
   if improved: segment_improved=True; break inner
```
But `try_pair_move` force only uses side if max_pd or min_pd sign but if mixed skip; could still if one side slightly. Not much. We can skip.

Maybe `active` from E selection can miss pairs where exact active set different. Fallback gradient top uses smooth weights; if top candidates exact derivative not side, skip. Hmm.

Alternative: In no improvement segment, instead of breaking, lower active_sel_tol to include more near-active? We used strict. If active one but top line rejected because line optimum no descent, true. If active fewer due stale E, maybe. Rebuild next segment? If no improvement and no h change, no need.

Thus break okay.

Now, `optimize_candidate` after pair no improvements can L-BFGS/perturb, then pair again. Good.

Now, final code. Let's proceed to final. Ensure fenced code only.
</think>

```python
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time
import math


def compute_c5_exact(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / n))


def is_feasible_raw(h, n):
    target = 0.5 * n
    return (
        float(np.min(h)) >= -1e-12
        and float(np.max(h)) <= 1.0 + 1e-12
        and abs(float(h.sum()) - target) <= 1e-8
    )


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    for _ in range(300):
        s = float(h.sum())
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-15:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = float(room.sum())
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * room
        else:
            tot = float(h.sum())
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * h
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    for _ in range(30):
        s = float(h.sum())
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            add = min(diff, 1.0 - h[idx])
            if add <= 0.0:
                break
            h[idx] += add
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(h))
            sub = min(-diff, h[idx])
            if sub <= 0.0:
                break
            h[idx] -= sub

    return h


def project_sym_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    for _ in range(3):
        h = 0.5 * (h + h[::-1])
        h = project_feasible(h, n)
    return h


def resample_h(h, n_old, n_new):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if n_old == n_new:
        return h.copy()
    x_old = (np.arange(n_old) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n_old)
    x_new = (np.arange(n_new) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n_new)
    hi = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h, left=h[0], right=h[-1])
    return project_feasible(hi, n_new)


def make_c(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")


def add_effect(h, n, i, sign, dest):
    L = 2 * n - 1

    dest[n - 1] += sign * (1.0 - 2.0 * h[i])

    fm = n - 1 - i
    if fm > 0:
        dest[n : n + fm] += sign * (1.0 - h[i + 1 : i + fm + 1])

    if i > 0:
        dest[n : n + i] -= sign * h[i - 1 :: -1]

    if i > 0:
        dest[n - 1 - i : n - 1] += sign * (1.0 - h[0:i])

    fm = n - 1 - i
    if fm > 0:
        dest[i : n - 1] -= sign * h[i + fm : i : -1]


def build_effect_matrix(h, n):
    L = 2 * n - 1
    E = np.zeros((n, L), dtype=float)
    for i in range(n):
        add_effect(h, n, i, 1.0, E[i])
    return E


def pair_value(delta, lin, c, idx1, idx2, d):
    v = c + delta * lin
    if d != 0:
        dd = delta * delta
        v[idx1] += dd
        v[idx2] += dd
    return float(np.max(v))


def pair_sg_delta(delta, lin, c, idx1, idx2, d, act_tol):
    v = c + delta * lin
    if d != 0:
        dd = delta * delta
        v[idx1] += dd
        v[idx2] += dd
    val = float(np.max(v))
    active = v >= (val - act_tol)
    sg = lin.copy()
    if d != 0:
        t = 2.0 * delta
        sg[idx1] += t
        sg[idx2] += t
    sgv = sg[active]
    return float(np.min(sgv)), float(np.max(sgv)), val


def try_pair_move(h, c, n, i, j, cmax, max_evals=32, act_tol=None, force=False):
    if i == j:
        return False, None

    hi = float(h[i])
    hj = float(h[j])

    low = max(-hi, hj - 1.0)
    high = min(hj, 1.0 - hi)
    if high - low < 1e-13:
        return False, None

    L = 2 * n - 1
    d = abs(i - j)
    idx1 = n - 1 - d
    idx2 = n - 1 + d

    lin = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    add_effect(h, n, i, 1.0, lin)
    add_effect(h, n, j, -1.0, lin)

    if act_tol is None:
        act_tol = max(1e-9, 1e-9 * abs(float(cmax)))

    active = np.flatnonzero(c >= (cmax - act_tol))
    if active.size == 0:
        active = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)

    lav = lin[active]
    max_pd = float(np.max(lav))
    min_pd = float(np.min(lav))

    side = 0
    if max_pd < -1e-11:
        side = 1
    elif min_pd > 1e-11:
        side = -1
    else:
        if not force:
            return False, None
        if max_pd < -1e-13:
            side = 1
        elif min_pd > 1e-13:
            side = -1
        else:
            return False, None

    if side > 0:
        a = 0.0
        b = float(high)
        if b <= a + 1e-13:
            return False, None
    else:
        a = float(low)
        b = 0.0
        if b <= a + 1e-13:
            return False, None

    best_delta = 0.0
    best_val = float(cmax)

    val_a = pair_value(a, lin, c, idx1, idx2, d)
    if val_a < best_val:
        best_val = val_a
        best_delta = a

    val_b = pair_value(b, lin, c, idx1, idx2, d)
    if val_b < best_val:
        best_val = val_b
        best_delta = b

    for _ in range(max_evals):
        if b - a <= 1e-12:
            break
        m = 0.5 * (a + b)
        sgn_min, sgn_max, val = pair_sg_delta(m, lin, c, idx1, idx2, d, act_tol)
        if val < best_val:
            best_val = val
            best_delta = m

        if sgn_min <= 1e-9 and sgn_max >= -1e-9:
            break
        if sgn_max < -1e-9:
            a = m
        elif sgn_min > 1e-9:
            b = m
        else:
            break

    candidates = (0.0, low, high, a, b, best_delta)
    for delta in candidates:
        if delta < low - 1e-12 or delta > high + 1e-12:
            continue
        if abs(delta) < 1e-14:
            continue
        val = pair_value(delta, lin, c, idx1, idx2, d)
        if val < best_val:
            best_val = val
            best_delta = delta

    if best_val >= float(cmax) - 1e-8:
        return False, None

    delta = float(best_delta)
    if delta > high:
        delta = high
    if delta < low:
        delta = low
    if abs(delta) < 1e-13:
        return False, None

    new_i = hi + delta
    new_j = hj - delta

    if new_i < 0.0:
        new_i = 0.0
        delta = hi
        new_j = hj - delta
    elif new_i > 1.0:
        new_i = 1.0
        delta = 1.0 - hi
        new_j = hj - delta

    if new_j < 0.0:
        new_j = 0.0
        delta = hj
        new_i = hi + delta
    elif new_j > 1.0:
        new_j = 1.0
        delta = hj - 1.0
        new_i = hi + delta

    actual = float(delta)
    if abs(actual) < 1e-13:
        return False, None

    c_trial = c + actual * lin
    if d != 0:
        dd = actual * actual
        c_trial[idx1] += dd
        c_trial[idx2] += dd

    nm = float(np.max(c_trial))
    if nm < float(cmax) - 1e-8:
        h[i] = new_i
        h[j] = new_j
        c[:] = c_trial
        return True, nm

    return False, None


def pair_optimizer(h0, n, deadline, rng=None):
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.RandomState(12345)

    h = project_feasible(h0, n)
    c = make_c(h, n)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = cmax

    if time.time() >= deadline:
        return best_h, best_c

    while time.time() < deadline:
        seg_start = time.time()
        seg_end = min(deadline, seg_start + 30.0)

        E = None
        segment_improved = False

        while time.time() < seg_end:
            cmax = float(np.max(c))

            if cmax < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_c = cmax
                best_h = h.copy()
                segment_improved = True

            if cmax > best_c + 1e-6:
                h = best_h.copy()
                c = make_c(h, n)
                cmax = float(np.max(c))
                E = None
                break

            sel_tol = max(1e-8, 1e-9 * abs(cmax))
            active = np.flatnonzero(c >= (cmax - sel_tol))
            if active.size == 0:
                active = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)

            if n > 1:
                maxA = max(1, min(8, 1200000 // (n * n)))
            else:
                maxA = 1
            if active.size > maxA:
                active = active[np.argsort(c[active])[-maxA:]]

            if E is None:
                E = build_effect_matrix(h, n)

            top_candidates = []
            A = len(active)

            if n > 1 and (n * n * A) <= 1200000:
                Ea = E[:, active]
                diff = Ea[None, :, :] - Ea[:, None, :]
                score = np.maximum(-diff.max(axis=2), diff.min(axis=2))

                iu, ju = np.triu_indices(n, 1)
                score_tri = score[iu, ju]
                k = min(400, score_tri.size)

                if k > 0:
                    if k == score_tri.size:
                        order = np.argsort(-score_tri)
                    else:
                        part = np.argpartition(-score_tri, k - 1)[:k]
                        order = part[np.argsort(-score_tri[part])]

                    for p in order:
                        if score_tri[p] > 1e-8:
                            top_candidates.append((int(iu[p]), int(ju[p])))
                        else:
                            break

            if len(top_candidates) == 0 and n > 1:
                y = c * (2.0 / n)
                w = None
                sm = float(np.max(y))
                s = 50000.0 * (y - sm)
                e = np.exp(s)
                w = e / float(e.sum())
                g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

                for m in range(2 * n - 1):
                    ww = float(w[m])
                    if ww < 1e-13:
                        continue
                    klag = m - (n - 1)
                    if klag >= 0:
                        nv = n - klag
                        g[0:nv] += ww * (1.0 - h[klag:n])
                        g[klag:n] += ww * (-h[0:nv])
                    else:
                        kk = -klag
                        nv = n - kk
                        g[kk:n] += ww * (1.0 - h[0:nv])
                        g[0:nv] += ww * (-h[kk:n])

                order = np.argsort(g)
                take = min(30, n // 3 + 1)
                low_idx = order[:take]
                high_idx = order[-take:]

                for aidx in low_idx:
                    for bidx in high_idx:
                        if aidx != bidx:
                            top_candidates.append((int(aidx), int(bidx)))

                if len(top_candidates) == 0:
                    for _ in range(50):
                        ii = int(rng.randint(n))
                        jj = int(rng.randint(n))
                        if ii != jj:
                            top_candidates.append((ii, jj))

            if len(top_candidates) == 0:
                break

            processed = 0
            accepted_segment = 0

            for ii, jj in top_candidates:
                if time.time() >= seg_end:
                    break

                if (h[ii] <= 1e-15 and h[jj] <= 1e-15) or (
                    h[ii] >= 1.0 - 1e-15 and h[jj] >= 1.0 - 1e-15
                ):
                    continue

                improved, cnew = try_pair_move(
                    h, c, n, ii, jj, cmax, max_evals=32, act_tol=sel_tol
                )

                if improved:
                    cmax = float(cnew)
                    if cmax < best_c - 1e-14:
                        best_c = cmax
                        best_h = h.copy()
                        segment_improved = True

                    accepted_segment += 1
                    if accepted_segment >= 20:
                        cdir = make_c(h, n)
                        ma = float(np.max(cdir))
                        if ma < cmax - 1e-12:
                            c = cdir
                            cmax = ma
                            if cmax < best_c - 1e-14:
                                best_c = cmax
                                best_h = h.copy()
                                segment_improved = True
                        elif ma > best_c + 1e-6:
                            h = best_h.copy()
                            c = make_c(h, n)
                            cmax = float(np.max(c))
                        E = None
                        break

                processed += 1
                if processed >= 800:
                    break

            if accepted_segment == 0:
                break

        if not segment_improved:
            break

    cdir = make_c(best_h, n)
    best_c = float(np.max(cdir))
    return best_h, best_c


def smooth_max_c5(x, n, alpha):
    c = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
    y = c * (2.0 / n)
    m = float(np.max(y))
    s = alpha * (y - m)
    return m + float(np.log(np.sum(np.exp(s)))) / alpha


def _lbfgs_obj(x, n, alpha, mu):
    val = smooth_max_c5(x, n, alpha)
    target = 0.5 * n
    val += mu * (float(x.sum()) - target) ** 2
    return float(val)


def _lbfgs_grad(x, n, alpha, mu):
    c = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
    y = c * (2.0 / n)
    m = float(np.max(y))
    s = alpha * (y - m)
    e = np.exp(s)
    w = e / float(e.sum())

    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for mi in range(2 * n - 1):
        ww = float(w[mi])
        if ww < 1e-13:
            continue
        k = mi - (n - 1)
        if k >= 0:
            nv = n - k
            g[0:nv] += ww * (1.0 - x[k:n])
            g[k:n] += ww * (-x[0:nv])
        else:
            kk = -k
            nv = n - kk
            g[kk:n] += ww * (1.0 - x[0:nv])
            g[0:nv] += ww * (-x[kk:n])

    g *= 2.0 / n
    target = 0.5 * n
    g += 2.0 * mu * (float(x.sum()) - target)
    return g


def lbfgs_phase(h0, n, deadline):
    h = project_feasible(h0, n)
    c = make_c(h, n)
    best_cs = float(np.max(c))
    best_h = h.copy()

    if time.time() >= deadline or n > 800:
        return best_h, best_cs

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    configs = [
        (500.0, 10.0),
        (5000.0, 50.0),
        (50000.0, 200.0),
    ]
    if n <= 400:
        configs.append((500000.0, 1000.0))

    for alpha, mu in configs:
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break

        maxiter = 80 if n <= 400 else 40
        try:
            res = minimize(
                _lbfgs_obj,
                h,
                jac=_lbfgs_grad,
                args=(n, alpha, mu),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={
                    "maxiter": maxiter,
                    "ftol": 1e-15,
                    "gtol": 1e-8,
                    "maxfun": maxiter * 10,
                    "maxls": 50,
                },
            )
            hnew = project_feasible(res.x, n)
            cnew = make_c(hnew, n)
            cs = float(np.max(cnew))
            if cs < best_cs - 1e-12:
                best_cs = cs
                best_h = hnew.copy()
                h = hnew
        except Exception:
            pass

    return best_h, best_cs


def optimize_candidate(h0, n, deadline, rng=None):
    h = project_feasible(h0, n)
    c = make_c(h, n)
    cs0 = float(np.max(c))
    c50 = cs0 * (2.0 / n)

    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c5 = c50
    best_cs = cs0

    if time.time() >= deadline:
        return best_h, best_c5

    t1 = min(deadline, time.time() + 150.0)
    h1, cs1 = pair_optimizer(h, n, t1, rng)
    c51 = cs1 * (2.0 / n)
    if c51 < best_c5 - 1e-15:
        best_h = h1
        best_c5 = c51
        best_cs = cs1

    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.RandomState(777)

    if n <= 800 and deadline - time.time() > 50.0:
        t2 = min(deadline, time.time() + 35.0)
        h2, cs2 = lbfgs_phase(best_h, n, t2)
        c52 = cs2 * (2.0 / n)
        if c52 < best_c5 - 1e-15:
            best_h = h2
            best_c5 = c52
            best_cs = cs2

        if deadline - time.time() > 25.0:
            noise = 0.003 if n <= 500 else 0.001
            hp = best_h + noise * (rng.standard_normal(n) - 0.5)
            hp = project_feasible(hp, n)
            t3 = min(deadline, time.time() + 20.0)
            h3, cs3 = lbfgs_phase(hp, n, t3)
            c53 = cs3 * (2.0 / n)
            if c53 < best_c5 - 1e-15:
                best_h = h3
                best_c5 = c53
                best_cs = cs3

    h4, cs4 = pair_optimizer(best_h, n, deadline, rng)
    c54 = cs4 * (2.0 / n)
    if c54 < best_c5 - 1e-15:
        best_h = h4
        best_c5 = c54
        best_cs = cs4

    return best_h, best_c5


def make_starts(n, rng=None):
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    starts = []

    starts.append(np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float))

    tri = np.clip(1.0 - np.abs(x - 1.0), 0.0, 1.0)
    starts.append(tri.copy())
    starts.append(np.power(tri, 0.5))
    starts.append(np.power(tri, 2.0))
    starts.append(np.power(tri, 4.0))

    cosv = np.clip(0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(np.pi * (x - 1.0)), 0.0, 1.0)
    starts.append(cosv)

    gauss = np.exp(-2.0 * (x - 1.0) * (x - 1.0))
    starts.append(gauss)

    if rng is not None:
        amps = (0.03, 0.08, 0.15)
        for a in amps:
            h = 0.5 + a * (rng.standard_normal(n) - 0.5)
            starts.append(h)

    out = []
    for h in starts:
        out.append(project_feasible(h, n))
    return out


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 8.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n0 = int(len(h0))
    except Exception:
        h0 = np.full(300, 0.5, dtype=float)
        n0 = 300

    if n0 < 2:
        h0 = np.full(2, 0.5, dtype=float)
        n0 = 2

    h0 = project_feasible(h0, n0)
    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5_exact(best_h, n0)
    best_n = n0

    hs = project_sym_feasible(h0, n0)
    cs = compute_c5_exact(hs, n0)
    if cs < best_c:
        best_h = hs.copy()
        best_c = cs
        best_n = n0

    reserve = max(120.0, 0.30 * float(budget_s))
    main_deadline = deadline - reserve
    if main_deadline < t0 + 20.0:
        main_deadline = deadline - 2.0

    h1, c1 = optimize_candidate(best_h, best_n, main_deadline, rng)
    hp = project_feasible(h1, best_n)
    cp = compute_c5_exact(hp, best_n)
    if cp < best_c:
        best_h = hp.copy()
        best_c = cp
        best_n = len(hp)

    if is_feasible_raw(h1, best_n):
        cr = compute_c5_exact(h1, best_n)
        if cr < best_c:
            best_h = h1.copy()
            best_c = cr

    if time.time() < deadline - 30.0:
        test_candidates = []
        cur = best_n
        for d in (1, 2, 3, 5, 10, 20, max(1, cur // 10)):
            for m in (cur - d, cur + d):
                if 20 <= m <= 1000 and m != best_n:
                    if m not in test_candidates:
                        test_candidates.append(m)

        for m in (best_n // 2, best_n * 2, 150, 600):
            if 20 <= m <= 1000 and m != best_n:
                if m not in test_candidates:
                    test_candidates.append(m)

        remaining = max(0.0, deadline - time.time() - 20.0)
        max_tests = int(remaining // 35.0)
        max_tests = max(0, min(max_tests, 8))
        test_candidates = test_candidates[:max_tests]

        for n_try in test_candidates:
            if time.time() > deadline - 20.0:
                break
            hseed = resample_h(best_h, best_n, n_try)
            alloc = min(70.0, max(0.0, deadline - time.time() - 15.0))
            if alloc < 20.0:
                break
            ho, co = optimize_candidate(hseed, n_try, time.time() + alloc, rng)
            hp2 = project_feasible(ho, n_try)
            cp2 = compute_c5_exact(hp2, n_try)
            if cp2 < best_c:
                best_h = hp2.copy()
                best_c = cp2
                best_n = n_try
            elif is_feasible_raw(ho, n_try):
                cr2 = compute_c5_exact(ho, n_try)
                if cr2 < best_c:
                    best_h = ho.copy()
                    best_c = cr2
                    best_n = n_try

    if time.time() < deadline - 40.0:
        small_ns = []
        for n_try in (5, 7, 10, 15, 20, 30, 50, 100, 150):
            if n_try != best_n:
                small_ns.append(n_try)

        for n_try in small_ns:
            if time.time() > deadline - 20.0:
                break
            starts = make_starts(n_try, rng)
            vals = []
            for idx in range(len(starts)):
                vals.append((compute_c5_exact(starts[idx], n_try), idx))
            vals.sort()

            per = max(15.0, min(40.0, max(0.0, (deadline - time.time() - 30.0) / 2.0)))
            for take_idx in range(min(2, len(vals))):
                if time.time() > deadline - 15.0:
                    break
                sc, idx = vals[take_idx]
                alloc = min(per, max(0.0, deadline - time.time() - 10.0))
                if alloc < 12.0:
                    break
                hseed = starts[idx]
                ho, co = optimize_candidate(hseed, n_try, time.time() + alloc, rng)
                hp3 = project_feasible(ho, n_try)
                cp3 = compute_c5_exact(hp3, n_try)
                if cp3 < best_c:
                    best_h = hp3.copy()
                    best_c = cp3
                    best_n = n_try
                elif is_feasible_raw(ho, n_try):
                    cr3 = compute_c5_exact(ho, n_try)
                    if cr3 < best_c:
                        best_h = ho.copy()
                        best_c = cr3
                        best_n = n_try

    if time.time() < deadline - 5.0:
        ho, co = pair_optimizer(best_h, best_n, deadline - 2.0, rng)
        hp4 = project_feasible(ho, best_n)
        cp4 = compute_c5_exact(hp4, best_n)
        if cp4 < best_c:
            best_h = hp4.copy()
            best_c = cp4
        elif is_feasible_raw(ho, best_n):
            cr4 = compute_c5_exact(ho, best_n)
            if cr4 < best_c:
                best_h = ho.copy()
                best_c = cr4

    h_final = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    c_final = compute_c5_exact(h_final, best_n)

    if is_feasible_raw(best_h, best_n):
        c_raw = compute_c5_exact(best_h, best_n)
        if c_raw < c_final:
            h_final = best_h.copy()
            c_final = c_raw

    return h_final, float(c_final), int(len(h_final))
```
